{"parentUuid":"4cd8627b-d789-4141-95e6-4c6229d01705","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a35f1a5a89ceb1af1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016JKiJqptoetMVeiwaN3raR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// GOOGLE OAUTH HELPER\n2\t// Mints a short-lived Google Cloud access token from a service-account JSON\n3\t// credential and caches it in Upstash Redis so the per-request cost is only\n4\t// paid once per warm-up window. Used by the Google Chirp 3 STT provider AND\n5\t// the GCS scratch bucket (lib/gcs-storage.ts) — both share one token.\n6\t//\n7\t// Cache key: `google_oauth_token`\n8\t// TTL: derived from Google's returned `expiry_date` (capped at 1h), expiring\n9\t//      10 minutes early so stragglers don't race the boundary. If Google\n10\t//      doesn't return an expiry, we fall back to a conservative 50 min TTL.\n11\t//\n12\t// Concurrency: a single in-flight Promise is shared so a cold-cache request\n13\t// burst (e.g. machine cold-start after deploy) mints exactly one token\n14\t// instead of N. Each region/machine still has its own JWT client + in-flight\n15\t// state — that's by design; cross-region coordination is what Redis is for.\n16\t\n17\timport { JWT } from 'google-auth-library';\n18\timport { redis } from './redis';\n19\t\n20\tconst TOKEN_CACHE_KEY = 'google_oauth_token';\n21\tconst FALLBACK_TTL_SECONDS = 3000;            // 50 min — used when Google omits expiry_date\n22\tconst TOKEN_TTL_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS = 600;  // expire 10 min before Google's stated expiry\n23\tconst MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 60;             // never cache shorter than 60s — pathological cases\n24\tconst SPEECH_SCOPE = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform';\n25\t\n26\tlet _jwtClient: JWT | null = null;\n27\tlet _inflight: Promise<string> | null = null;\n28\t\n29\tfunction getJwtClient(): JWT {\n30\t  if (_jwtClient) {\n31\t    return _jwtClient;\n32\t  }\n33\t\n34\t  const raw = process.env.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON;\n35\t  if (!raw) {\n36\t    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON not configured');\n37\t  }\n38\t\n39\t  let credentials: { client_email?: string; private_key?: string };\n40\t  try {\n41\t    credentials = JSON.parse(raw);\n42\t  } catch (error) {\n43\t    throw new Error(`GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is not valid JSON: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);\n44\t  }\n45\t\n46\t  if (!credentials.client_email || !credentials.private_key) {\n47\t    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is missing client_email or private_key');\n48\t  }\n49\t\n50\t  _jwtClient = new JWT({\n51\t    email: credentials.client_email,\n52\t    key: credentials.private_key,\n53\t    scopes: [SPEECH_SCOPE],\n54\t  });\n55\t\n56\t  return _jwtClient;\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\tfunction computeCacheTtlSeconds(expiryDate: number | null | undefined): number {\n60\t  if (typeof expiryDate !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(expiryDate)) {\n61\t    return FALLBACK_TTL_SECONDS;\n62\t  }\n63\t  const remainingSeconds = Math.floor((expiryDate - Date.now()) / 1000) - TOKEN_TTL_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS;\n64\t  if (remainingSeconds < MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS) {\n65\t    // Pathological — Google handed us a token already at/near expiry. Cache\n66\t    // briefly so a burst doesn't re-mint per request but expire fast so the\n67\t    // next regular request gets a fresh one.\n68\t    return MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS;\n69\t  }\n70\t  return remainingSeconds;\n71\t}\n72\t\n73\tasync function mintAndCacheToken(): Promise<string> {\n74\t  const jwt = getJwtClient();\n75\t\n76\t  let access_token: string | null | undefined;\n77\t  let expiry_date: number | null | undefined;\n78\t  try {\n79\t    ({ access_token, expiry_date } = await jwt.authorize());\n80\t  } catch (error) {\n81\t    // Singleton failure surface — this is the entire health of the Chirp\n82\t    // self-only chain hanging on it, so the log line matters.\n83\t    console.error('google-auth.token_mint_failed', {\n84\t      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n85\t    });\n86\t    // Clear inflight on failure so the next request retries instead of\n87\t    // hanging on a permanently-rejected promise.\n88\t    _inflight = null;\n89\t    throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));\n90\t  }\n91\t\n92\t  if (!access_token) {\n93\t    const err = new Error('Google service account did not return an access_token');\n94\t    console.error('google-auth.token_mint_failed', { message: err.message });\n95\t    _inflight = null;\n96\t    throw err;\n97\t  }\n98\t\n99\t  const ttlSeconds = computeCacheTtlSeconds(expiry_date);\n100\t\n101\t  try {\n102\t    await redis.get().set(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY, access_token, { ex: ttlSeconds });\n103\t  } catch (error) {\n104\t    // Cache-write failure isn't fatal — the caller already has a usable\n105\t    // token. The next request just pays the mint cost again.\n106\t    console.warn('google-auth.cache_write_failed', {\n107\t      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n108\t      ttlSeconds,\n109\t    });\n110\t  }\n111\t\n112\t  // Only clear `_inflight` AFTER the Redis SET attempt resolves. Clearing\n113\t  // earlier would let a concurrent caller arrive between mint-completion\n114\t  // and cache-write, see no inflight, miss the cache, and mint a second\n115\t  // token. `mintAndCacheToken` owns its own inflight lifecycle now.\n116\t  _inflight = null;\n117\t  return access_token;\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/**\n121\t * Get a Google Cloud OAuth access token, using the Upstash Redis cache when\n122\t * possible. Concurrent cold-cache callers share a single in-flight mint via\n123\t * `_inflight` so we don't fan out N parallel `authorize()` calls under burst.\n124\t */\n125\texport async function getGoogleAccessToken(): Promise<string> {\n126\t  try {\n127\t    const cached = await redis.get().get<string>(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY);\n128\t    if (typeof cached === 'string' && cached.length > 0) {\n129\t      return cached;\n130\t    }\n131\t  } catch (error) {\n132\t    console.warn('google-auth.cache_read_failed', {\n133\t      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n134\t    });\n135\t  }\n136\t\n137\t  if (_inflight) {\n138\t    return _inflight;\n139\t  }\n140\t\n141\t  // Assign before awaiting so concurrent callers see the inflight promise.\n142\t  // `mintAndCacheToken` is responsible for clearing `_inflight` itself —\n143\t  // the clear must happen after the Redis SET attempt resolves, not in a\n144\t  // wrapping `.finally` on this side (see note inside that function).\n145\t  _inflight = mintAndCacheToken();\n146\t\n147\t  return _inflight;\n148\t}\n149\t\n150\t/**\n151\t * Force-invalidate the cached Google access token. Used when an in-flight\n152\t * request hits a 401 mid-poll because the cached token expired faster than\n153\t * its declared TTL — the next `getGoogleAccessToken` call after this will\n154\t * re-mint from the JWT client instead of returning a stale Redis hit.\n155\t */\n156\texport async function invalidateGoogleAccessToken(): Promise<void> {\n157\t  try {\n158\t    await redis.get().del(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY);\n159\t  } catch (error) {\n160\t    console.warn('google-auth.cache_delete_failed', {\n161\t      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n162\t    });\n163\t  }\n164\t  _inflight = null;\n165\t}\n166\t"}]},"uuid":"a5634829-ecb7-4fee-b856-52d78eea7832","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:39:32.984Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/google-auth.ts","content":"// GOOGLE OAUTH HELPER\n// Mints a short-lived Google Cloud access token from a service-account JSON\n// credential and caches it in Upstash Redis so the per-request cost is only\n// paid once per warm-up window. Used by the Google Chirp 3 STT provider AND\n// the GCS scratch bucket (lib/gcs-storage.ts) — both share one token.\n//\n// Cache key: `google_oauth_token`\n// TTL: derived from Google's returned `expiry_date` (capped at 1h), expiring\n//      10 minutes early so stragglers don't race the boundary. If Google\n//      doesn't return an expiry, we fall back to a conservative 50 min TTL.\n//\n// Concurrency: a single in-flight Promise is shared so a cold-cache request\n// burst (e.g. machine cold-start after deploy) mints exactly one token\n// instead of N. Each region/machine still has its own JWT client + in-flight\n// state — that's by design; cross-region coordination is what Redis is for.\n\nimport { JWT } from 'google-auth-library';\nimport { redis } from './redis';\n\nconst TOKEN_CACHE_KEY = 'google_oauth_token';\nconst FALLBACK_TTL_SECONDS = 3000;            // 50 min — used when Google omits expiry_date\nconst TOKEN_TTL_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS = 600;  // expire 10 min before Google's stated expiry\nconst MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 60;             // never cache shorter than 60s — pathological cases\nconst SPEECH_SCOPE = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform';\n\nlet _jwtClient: JWT | null = null;\nlet _inflight: Promise<string> | null = null;\n\nfunction getJwtClient(): JWT {\n  if (_jwtClient) {\n    return _jwtClient;\n  }\n\n  const raw = process.env.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON;\n  if (!raw) {\n    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON not configured');\n  }\n\n  let credentials: { client_email?: string; private_key?: string };\n  try {\n    credentials = JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch (error) {\n    throw new Error(`GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is not valid JSON: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);\n  }\n\n  if (!credentials.client_email || !credentials.private_key) {\n    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is missing client_email or private_key');\n  }\n\n  _jwtClient = new JWT({\n    email: credentials.client_email,\n    key: credentials.private_key,\n    scopes: [SPEECH_SCOPE],\n  });\n\n  return _jwtClient;\n}\n\nfunction computeCacheTtlSeconds(expiryDate: number | null | undefined): number {\n  if (typeof expiryDate !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(expiryDate)) {\n    return FALLBACK_TTL_SECONDS;\n  }\n  const remainingSeconds = Math.floor((expiryDate - Date.now()) / 1000) - TOKEN_TTL_SAFETY_MARGIN_SECONDS;\n  if (remainingSeconds < MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS) {\n    // Pathological — Google handed us a token already at/near expiry. Cache\n    // briefly so a burst doesn't re-mint per request but expire fast so the\n    // next regular request gets a fresh one.\n    return MIN_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS;\n  }\n  return remainingSeconds;\n}\n\nasync function mintAndCacheToken(): Promise<string> {\n  const jwt = getJwtClient();\n\n  let access_token: string | null | undefined;\n  let expiry_date: number | null | undefined;\n  try {\n    ({ access_token, expiry_date } = await jwt.authorize());\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Singleton failure surface — this is the entire health of the Chirp\n    // self-only chain hanging on it, so the log line matters.\n    console.error('google-auth.token_mint_failed', {\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n    // Clear inflight on failure so the next request retries instead of\n    // hanging on a permanently-rejected promise.\n    _inflight = null;\n    throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));\n  }\n\n  if (!access_token) {\n    const err = new Error('Google service account did not return an access_token');\n    console.error('google-auth.token_mint_failed', { message: err.message });\n    _inflight = null;\n    throw err;\n  }\n\n  const ttlSeconds = computeCacheTtlSeconds(expiry_date);\n\n  try {\n    await redis.get().set(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY, access_token, { ex: ttlSeconds });\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Cache-write failure isn't fatal — the caller already has a usable\n    // token. The next request just pays the mint cost again.\n    console.warn('google-auth.cache_write_failed', {\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n      ttlSeconds,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Only clear `_inflight` AFTER the Redis SET attempt resolves. Clearing\n  // earlier would let a concurrent caller arrive between mint-completion\n  // and cache-write, see no inflight, miss the cache, and mint a second\n  // token. `mintAndCacheToken` owns its own inflight lifecycle now.\n  _inflight = null;\n  return access_token;\n}\n\n/**\n * Get a Google Cloud OAuth access token, using the Upstash Redis cache when\n * possible. Concurrent cold-cache callers share a single in-flight mint via\n * `_inflight` so we don't fan out N parallel `authorize()` calls under burst.\n */\nexport async function getGoogleAccessToken(): Promise<string> {\n  try {\n    const cached = await redis.get().get<string>(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY);\n    if (typeof cached === 'string' && cached.length > 0) {\n      return cached;\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('google-auth.cache_read_failed', {\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (_inflight) {\n    return _inflight;\n  }\n\n  // Assign before awaiting so concurrent callers see the inflight promise.\n  // `mintAndCacheToken` is responsible for clearing `_inflight` itself —\n  // the clear must happen after the Redis SET attempt resolves, not in a\n  // wrapping `.finally` on this side (see note inside that function).\n  _inflight = mintAndCacheToken();\n\n  return _inflight;\n}\n\n/**\n * Force-invalidate the cached Google access token. Used when an in-flight\n * request hits a 401 mid-poll because the cached token expired faster than\n * its declared TTL — the next `getGoogleAccessToken` call after this will\n * re-mint from the JWT client instead of returning a stale Redis hit.\n */\nexport async function invalidateGoogleAccessToken(): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await redis.get().del(TOKEN_CACHE_KEY);\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('google-auth.cache_delete_failed', {\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n  _inflight = null;\n}\n","numLines":166,"startLine":1,"totalLines":166}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4cd8627b-d789-4141-95e6-4c6229d01705","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"a9930849-a127-4227-8e71-db1706ce9edb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a35f1a5a89ceb1af1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E6LmAQNEwdCPtgvbtqhjT7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// GCS STORAGE (transcription scratch path)\n2\t//\n3\t// Tightly scoped service for one purpose: upload an audio payload to the\n4\t// HyperWhisper Cloud transcription bucket so Google Speech V2 can read it via\n5\t// a `gs://` URI, then delete the object after transcription completes. The\n6\t// module knows nothing about Chirp, Speech V2, or the transcribe route — it\n7\t// only knows how to PUT bytes to a bucket and DELETE them again.\n8\t//\n9\t// Lifecycle contract:\n10\t//   1. caller invokes `uploadTranscriptionAudio` → receives { gcsUri, ref }\n11\t//   2. caller passes `gcsUri` to Google Speech V2 in the `uri` field\n12\t//   3. caller invokes `deleteTranscriptionAudio(ref)` in a `finally` block\n13\t//\n14\t// Defense in depth: the bucket SHOULD also have a server-side lifecycle rule\n15\t// (Delete after 1 day) so an orphaned object from a worker crash or\n16\t// network failure between recognize and delete still gets cleaned up.\n17\t// See hyperwhisper-cloud/CLAUDE.md for the bucket setup playbook.\n18\t//\n19\t// Why a `gs://` URI not a public-read URL: Speech V2 reads from GCS via the\n20\t// service account's IAM, never via a public URL. The bucket can stay private\n21\t// (and SHOULD — these are user voice recordings).\n22\t\n23\timport { getGoogleAccessToken } from './google-auth';\n24\timport { ProviderUnavailableError } from '../providers/types';\n25\t\n26\tconst GCS_API_BASE = 'https://storage.googleapis.com';\n27\tconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS = 30_000;\n28\tconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS = 1_000;\n29\tconst DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n30\t\n31\t/**\n32\t * Scale upload budget with payload size so a slow-region 50–100 MB upload\n33\t * doesn't time out at 30 s. Floor stays at 30 s for small uploads, +1 s per\n34\t * 100 KB on top. A 100 MB file gets ~1000 s, 2 GB (the upstream max) ~20 000 s.\n35\t */\n36\tfunction computeUploadTimeoutMs(byteLength: number): number {\n37\t  return Math.max(UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS, Math.ceil(byteLength / 100_000) * UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS);\n38\t}\n39\t\n40\t/**\n41\t * Opaque reference for a temporary transcription object, returned by\n42\t * `uploadTranscriptionAudio` and consumed by `deleteTranscriptionAudio`.\n43\t * Keeping bucket + objectName paired prevents callers from mixing buckets.\n44\t */\n45\texport interface TranscriptionAudioRef {\n46\t  readonly bucket: string;\n47\t  readonly objectName: string;\n48\t}\n49\t\n50\texport interface TranscriptionAudioUpload extends TranscriptionAudioRef {\n51\t  /** `gs://<bucket>/<objectName>` — pass straight to Speech V2 `uri`. */\n52\t  readonly gcsUri: string;\n53\t}\n54\t\n55\tfunction getConfiguredBucket(): string | null {\n56\t  const bucket = process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET?.trim();\n57\t  return bucket && bucket.length > 0 ? bucket : null;\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\t/** True when `uploadTranscriptionAudio` can be used. */\n61\texport function isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured(): boolean {\n62\t  return getConfiguredBucket() !== null;\n63\t}\n64\t\n65\tfunction inferExtension(contentType: string): string {\n66\t  const lower = contentType.toLowerCase();\n67\t  if (lower.includes('wav')) return 'wav';\n68\t  if (lower.includes('mp3') || lower.includes('mpeg')) return 'mp3';\n69\t  if (lower.includes('m4a') || lower.includes('mp4')) return 'm4a';\n70\t  if (lower.includes('webm')) return 'webm';\n71\t  if (lower.includes('ogg') || lower.includes('opus')) return 'ogg';\n72\t  if (lower.includes('flac')) return 'flac';\n73\t  if (lower.includes('aac')) return 'aac';\n74\t  return 'bin';\n75\t}\n76\t\n77\tfunction buildObjectName(ext: string): string {\n78\t  // Bun and Node both expose crypto.randomUUID() globally.\n79\t  const uuid = crypto.randomUUID();\n80\t  // The `stt-temp/` prefix lets the bucket's lifecycle rule scope to\n81\t  // exactly this scratch path without affecting any other use of the bucket.\n82\t  return `stt-temp/${Date.now()}-${uuid}.${ext}`;\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\tasync function fetchWithTimeoutMs(url: string, init: RequestInit, timeoutMs: number): Promise<Response> {\n86\t  const controller = new AbortController();\n87\t  const handle = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);\n88\t  try {\n89\t    return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal: controller.signal });\n90\t  } finally {\n91\t    clearTimeout(handle);\n92\t  }\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\t/**\n96\t * PUT the audio into the configured bucket and return the gs:// URI plus\n97\t * the delete handle. Throws if the bucket isn't configured — callers should\n98\t * gate with `isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured()` first.\n99\t */\n100\texport async function uploadTranscriptionAudio(\n101\t  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n102\t  contentType: string,\n103\t): Promise<TranscriptionAudioUpload> {\n104\t  const bucket = getConfiguredBucket();\n105\t  if (!bucket) {\n106\t    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET not configured');\n107\t  }\n108\t\n109\t  const ext = inferExtension(contentType);\n110\t  const objectName = buildObjectName(ext);\n111\t  const accessToken = await getGoogleAccessToken();\n112\t\n113\t  // Simple media upload (single request, no resumable session). Audio\n114\t  // payloads here are capped well under 5 GB (the simple-upload limit) by\n115\t  // MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES upstream.\n116\t  const url = `${GCS_API_BASE}/upload/storage/v1/b/${encodeURIComponent(bucket)}/o`\n117\t    + `?uploadType=media&name=${encodeURIComponent(objectName)}`;\n118\t\n119\t  const start = performance.now();\n120\t  const timeoutMs = computeUploadTimeoutMs(audio.byteLength);\n121\t  let response: Response;\n122\t  try {\n123\t    response = await fetchWithTimeoutMs(url, {\n124\t      method: 'POST',\n125\t      headers: {\n126\t        'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}`,\n127\t        'Content-Type': contentType || 'application/octet-stream',\n128\t      },\n129\t      body: audio,\n130\t    }, timeoutMs);\n131\t  } catch (error) {\n132\t    // Route timeouts/network errors through ProviderUnavailableError so\n133\t    // transcribe.ts hits the 502 chain-fail path instead of returning 500.\n134\t    if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === 'AbortError') {\n135\t      throw new ProviderUnavailableError('GCS upload', `upload timeout after ${timeoutMs}ms`);\n136\t    }\n137\t    throw new ProviderUnavailableError(\n138\t      'GCS upload',\n139\t      `network error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,\n140\t    );\n141\t  }\n142\t\n143\t  const elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);\n144\t\n145\t  if (!response.ok) {\n146\t    const preview = await response.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');\n147\t    // Route transient GCS failures (429 throttling, 5xx outages) through\n148\t    // ProviderUnavailableError so transcribe.ts surfaces them as 502 via the\n149\t    // chain-fail path. Non-transient codes (403 bad IAM, 404 missing bucket,\n150\t    // 400 malformed request) stay as plain Errors so they fail fast instead\n151\t    // of silently cascading to alternate providers.\n152\t    if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {\n153\t      throw new ProviderUnavailableError(\n154\t        'GCS upload',\n155\t        `upload ${response.status} after ${elapsedMs}ms: ${preview.slice(0, 200)}`,\n156\t      );\n157\t    }\n158\t    throw new Error(\n159\t      `GCS upload failed (status=${response.status}, elapsedMs=${elapsedMs}): ${preview.slice(0, 400)}`,\n160\t    );\n161\t  }\n162\t\n163\t  console.log('gcs.upload_ok', {\n164\t    bucket,\n165\t    objectName,\n166\t    bytes: audio.byteLength,\n167\t    elapsedMs,\n168\t  });\n169\t\n170\t  return {\n171\t    bucket,\n172\t    objectName,\n173\t    gcsUri: `gs://${bucket}/${objectName}`,\n174\t  };\n175\t}\n176\t\n177\t/**\n178\t * Delete a temporary transcription object. Swallows all errors and logs them\n179\t * — the caller has already produced (or attempted) a transcript and should\n180\t * not surface a 5xx because cleanup failed. The bucket's lifecycle rule is\n181\t * the durable backstop for any object this miss.\n182\t */\n183\texport async function deleteTranscriptionAudio(ref: TranscriptionAudioRef): Promise<void> {\n184\t  const url = `${GCS_API_BASE}/storage/v1/b/${encodeURIComponent(ref.bucket)}/o/${encodeURIComponent(ref.objectName)}`;\n185\t\n186\t  try {\n187\t    const accessToken = await getGoogleAccessToken();\n188\t    const start = performance.now();\n189\t    const response = await fetchWithTimeoutMs(url, {\n190\t      method: 'DELETE',\n191\t      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}` },\n192\t    }, DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS);\n193\t\n194\t    const elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);\n195\t\n196\t    // 404 is fine — already gone (re-entrant cleanup or lifecycle race).\n197\t    if (!response.ok && response.status !== 404) {\n198\t      const preview = await response.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');\n199\t      console.warn('gcs.delete_failed', {\n200\t        bucket: ref.bucket,\n201\t        objectName: ref.objectName,\n202\t        status: response.status,\n203\t        elapsedMs,\n204\t        bodyPreview: preview.slice(0, 200),\n205\t      });\n206\t      return;\n207\t    }\n208\t\n209\t    console.log('gcs.delete_ok', {\n210\t      bucket: ref.bucket,\n211\t      objectName: ref.objectName,\n212\t      elapsedMs,\n213\t      status: response.status,\n214\t    });\n215\t  } catch (error) {\n216\t    console.warn('gcs.delete_error', {\n217\t      bucket: ref.bucket,\n218\t      objectName: ref.objectName,\n219\t      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n220\t    });\n221\t  }\n222\t}\n223\t"}]},"uuid":"fd2a1cab-8b58-46b3-9ca3-0e819b498a0f","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:39:33.498Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/gcs-storage.ts","content":"// GCS STORAGE (transcription scratch path)\n//\n// Tightly scoped service for one purpose: upload an audio payload to the\n// HyperWhisper Cloud transcription bucket so Google Speech V2 can read it via\n// a `gs://` URI, then delete the object after transcription completes. The\n// module knows nothing about Chirp, Speech V2, or the transcribe route — it\n// only knows how to PUT bytes to a bucket and DELETE them again.\n//\n// Lifecycle contract:\n//   1. caller invokes `uploadTranscriptionAudio` → receives { gcsUri, ref }\n//   2. caller passes `gcsUri` to Google Speech V2 in the `uri` field\n//   3. caller invokes `deleteTranscriptionAudio(ref)` in a `finally` block\n//\n// Defense in depth: the bucket SHOULD also have a server-side lifecycle rule\n// (Delete after 1 day) so an orphaned object from a worker crash or\n// network failure between recognize and delete still gets cleaned up.\n// See hyperwhisper-cloud/CLAUDE.md for the bucket setup playbook.\n//\n// Why a `gs://` URI not a public-read URL: Speech V2 reads from GCS via the\n// service account's IAM, never via a public URL. The bucket can stay private\n// (and SHOULD — these are user voice recordings).\n\nimport { getGoogleAccessToken } from './google-auth';\nimport { ProviderUnavailableError } from '../providers/types';\n\nconst GCS_API_BASE = 'https://storage.googleapis.com';\nconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS = 30_000;\nconst UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS = 1_000;\nconst DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;\n\n/**\n * Scale upload budget with payload size so a slow-region 50–100 MB upload\n * doesn't time out at 30 s. Floor stays at 30 s for small uploads, +1 s per\n * 100 KB on top. A 100 MB file gets ~1000 s, 2 GB (the upstream max) ~20 000 s.\n */\nfunction computeUploadTimeoutMs(byteLength: number): number {\n  return Math.max(UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_FLOOR_MS, Math.ceil(byteLength / 100_000) * UPLOAD_TIMEOUT_PER_100KB_MS);\n}\n\n/**\n * Opaque reference for a temporary transcription object, returned by\n * `uploadTranscriptionAudio` and consumed by `deleteTranscriptionAudio`.\n * Keeping bucket + objectName paired prevents callers from mixing buckets.\n */\nexport interface TranscriptionAudioRef {\n  readonly bucket: string;\n  readonly objectName: string;\n}\n\nexport interface TranscriptionAudioUpload extends TranscriptionAudioRef {\n  /** `gs://<bucket>/<objectName>` — pass straight to Speech V2 `uri`. */\n  readonly gcsUri: string;\n}\n\nfunction getConfiguredBucket(): string | null {\n  const bucket = process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET?.trim();\n  return bucket && bucket.length > 0 ? bucket : null;\n}\n\n/** True when `uploadTranscriptionAudio` can be used. */\nexport function isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured(): boolean {\n  return getConfiguredBucket() !== null;\n}\n\nfunction inferExtension(contentType: string): string {\n  const lower = contentType.toLowerCase();\n  if (lower.includes('wav')) return 'wav';\n  if (lower.includes('mp3') || lower.includes('mpeg')) return 'mp3';\n  if (lower.includes('m4a') || lower.includes('mp4')) return 'm4a';\n  if (lower.includes('webm')) return 'webm';\n  if (lower.includes('ogg') || lower.includes('opus')) return 'ogg';\n  if (lower.includes('flac')) return 'flac';\n  if (lower.includes('aac')) return 'aac';\n  return 'bin';\n}\n\nfunction buildObjectName(ext: string): string {\n  // Bun and Node both expose crypto.randomUUID() globally.\n  const uuid = crypto.randomUUID();\n  // The `stt-temp/` prefix lets the bucket's lifecycle rule scope to\n  // exactly this scratch path without affecting any other use of the bucket.\n  return `stt-temp/${Date.now()}-${uuid}.${ext}`;\n}\n\nasync function fetchWithTimeoutMs(url: string, init: RequestInit, timeoutMs: number): Promise<Response> {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  const handle = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);\n  try {\n    return await fetch(url, { ...init, signal: controller.signal });\n  } finally {\n    clearTimeout(handle);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * PUT the audio into the configured bucket and return the gs:// URI plus\n * the delete handle. Throws if the bucket isn't configured — callers should\n * gate with `isGcsTranscriptionBucketConfigured()` first.\n */\nexport async function uploadTranscriptionAudio(\n  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n  contentType: string,\n): Promise<TranscriptionAudioUpload> {\n  const bucket = getConfiguredBucket();\n  if (!bucket) {\n    throw new Error('GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET not configured');\n  }\n\n  const ext = inferExtension(contentType);\n  const objectName = buildObjectName(ext);\n  const accessToken = await getGoogleAccessToken();\n\n  // Simple media upload (single request, no resumable session). Audio\n  // payloads here are capped well under 5 GB (the simple-upload limit) by\n  // MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES upstream.\n  const url = `${GCS_API_BASE}/upload/storage/v1/b/${encodeURIComponent(bucket)}/o`\n    + `?uploadType=media&name=${encodeURIComponent(objectName)}`;\n\n  const start = performance.now();\n  const timeoutMs = computeUploadTimeoutMs(audio.byteLength);\n  let response: Response;\n  try {\n    response = await fetchWithTimeoutMs(url, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}`,\n        'Content-Type': contentType || 'application/octet-stream',\n      },\n      body: audio,\n    }, timeoutMs);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Route timeouts/network errors through ProviderUnavailableError so\n    // transcribe.ts hits the 502 chain-fail path instead of returning 500.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && error.name === 'AbortError') {\n      throw new ProviderUnavailableError('GCS upload', `upload timeout after ${timeoutMs}ms`);\n    }\n    throw new ProviderUnavailableError(\n      'GCS upload',\n      `network error: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  const elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);\n\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    const preview = await response.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');\n    // Route transient GCS failures (429 throttling, 5xx outages) through\n    // ProviderUnavailableError so transcribe.ts surfaces them as 502 via the\n    // chain-fail path. Non-transient codes (403 bad IAM, 404 missing bucket,\n    // 400 malformed request) stay as plain Errors so they fail fast instead\n    // of silently cascading to alternate providers.\n    if (response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) {\n      throw new ProviderUnavailableError(\n        'GCS upload',\n        `upload ${response.status} after ${elapsedMs}ms: ${preview.slice(0, 200)}`,\n      );\n    }\n    throw new Error(\n      `GCS upload failed (status=${response.status}, elapsedMs=${elapsedMs}): ${preview.slice(0, 400)}`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  console.log('gcs.upload_ok', {\n    bucket,\n    objectName,\n    bytes: audio.byteLength,\n    elapsedMs,\n  });\n\n  return {\n    bucket,\n    objectName,\n    gcsUri: `gs://${bucket}/${objectName}`,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Delete a temporary transcription object. Swallows all errors and logs them\n * — the caller has already produced (or attempted) a transcript and should\n * not surface a 5xx because cleanup failed. The bucket's lifecycle rule is\n * the durable backstop for any object this miss.\n */\nexport async function deleteTranscriptionAudio(ref: TranscriptionAudioRef): Promise<void> {\n  const url = `${GCS_API_BASE}/storage/v1/b/${encodeURIComponent(ref.bucket)}/o/${encodeURIComponent(ref.objectName)}`;\n\n  try {\n    const accessToken = await getGoogleAccessToken();\n    const start = performance.now();\n    const response = await fetchWithTimeoutMs(url, {\n      method: 'DELETE',\n      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}` },\n    }, DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS);\n\n    const elapsedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - start);\n\n    // 404 is fine — already gone (re-entrant cleanup or lifecycle race).\n    if (!response.ok && response.status !== 404) {\n      const preview = await response.text().catch(() => '<unreadable>');\n      console.warn('gcs.delete_failed', {\n        bucket: ref.bucket,\n        objectName: ref.objectName,\n        status: response.status,\n        elapsedMs,\n        bodyPreview: preview.slice(0, 200),\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    console.log('gcs.delete_ok', {\n      bucket: ref.bucket,\n      objectName: ref.objectName,\n      elapsedMs,\n      status: response.status,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('gcs.delete_error', {\n      bucket: ref.bucket,\n      objectName: ref.objectName,\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n","numLines":223,"startLine":1,"totalLines":223}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a9930849-a127-4227-8e71-db1706ce9edb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n53\t    } catch {\n54\t      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n58\t    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n59\t\n60\t    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n61\t\n62\t    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n63\t    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n64\t    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n65\t    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n66\t    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n67\t    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n68\t    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n69\t    // the licensing API on every call.\n70\t    if (response.status >= 500) {\n71\t      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n72\t      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n73\t    }\n74\t\n75\t    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n76\t      isValid,\n77\t      credits,\n78\t      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    return { isValid, credits };\n82\t  } catch (error) {\n83\t    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n84\t    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n85\t    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. There is no anonymous/trial\n101\t  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n102\t  if (!licenseKey) {\n103\t    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n104\t    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n105\t  }\n106\t\n107\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n108\t\n109\t  if (!forceRefresh) {\n110\t    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n111\t    if (cached) {\n112\t      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n113\t      if (!cached.isValid) {\n114\t        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n115\t      }\n116\t      return {\n117\t        ok: true,\n118\t        value: {\n119\t          identifier: licenseKey,\n120\t          credits: cached.credits,\n121\t          licenseKey,\n122\t        },\n123\t      };\n124\t    }\n125\t    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n126\t  } else {\n127\t    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n128\t  }\n129\t\n130\t  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n131\t  if (!validation.isValid) {\n132\t    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n133\t    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n134\t  }\n135\t\n136\t  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n137\t  return {\n138\t    ok: true,\n139\t    value: {\n140\t      identifier: licenseKey,\n141\t      credits: validation.credits,\n142\t      licenseKey,\n143\t    },\n144\t  };\n145\t}\n146\t"}]},"uuid":"8fd19b29-6462-4827-b140-9cd78c6134df","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:39:33.927Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts","content":"// AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n\nimport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\nimport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n\nexport interface AuthContext {\n  identifier: string; // license key\n  credits: number;\n  licenseKey: string;\n}\n\nexport interface AuthInput {\n  licenseKey?: string;\n}\n\nexport type AuthResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\n// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\nfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n    // the licensing API on every call.\n    if (response.status >= 500) {\n      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n    } else {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n    }\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n  const { licenseKey } = input;\n\n  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n      if (!cached.isValid) {\n        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n      }\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        value: {\n          identifier: licenseKey,\n          credits: cached.credits,\n          licenseKey,\n        },\n      };\n    }\n    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n  }\n\n  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n  if (!validation.isValid) {\n    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n  }\n\n  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    value: {\n      identifier: licenseKey,\n      credits: validation.credits,\n      licenseKey,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"33ec3974-f5cd-43f4-b1e3-ec20b4fd0e21","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

route---\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { validateCreditDeductionAmount } from \"./validation\";\n\nimport {\n  deductCreditBalance,\n  findAccountByKey,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * License Credits API\n *\n * Manages credit balance for licensed users.\n * Used by HyperWhisper Cloud (CF Workers) to check balance and deduct credits.\n *\n * ENDPOINTS:\n * - GET: Get credit balance for a license key\n * - POST: Deduct credits (record usage)\n *\n * SECURITY:\n * - The license key itself acts as authentication\n * - Only valid license keys can query/deduct credits\n */\n\n/**\n * GET /api/license/credits\n *\n * Get credit balance for a license key.\n */\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);\n  const licenseKey = searchParams.get(\"license_key\");\n\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits,\n      stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits balance error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Failed to get credit balance\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * POST /api/license/credits\n *\n * Deduct credits from a license (record usage).\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;\n\n    if (!license_key || typeof license_key !== \"string\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const amountError = validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount);\n\n    if (amountError) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(license_key.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    let newCredits: number;\n\n    try {\n      // Atomic SQL decrement (floored at 0) — concurrent POSTs cannot\n      // double-spend via a read-then-write race.\n      newCredits = await deductCreditBalance(license.userId, amount);\n    } catch (updateError) {\n      console.error(\"Credit deduction failed:\", updateError);\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Failed to deduct credits. Please retry.\" },\n        { status: 409 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    console.log(`Credits deducted: ${amount} from license ${license_key.substring(0, 7)}...`, {\n      remaining: newCredits,\n      metadata,\n    });\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits_remaining: newCredits,\n      credits_deducted: amount,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits deduction error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Failed to deduct credits\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"464c9752-fd6b-45d8-b4e5-9ab54d918b68","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:26.878Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"activate\ncredits\ndeactivate\nvalidate\n---credits {"parentUuid":"2db74ffb-4149-4883-aa6e-68292f568f8c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a35f1a5a89ceb1af1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018Cx3REAKmN5wqpZEhBpn3N","type":"tool_result","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport {\n  upsertDeviceValidation,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { checkLicenseKey } from \"@/src/lib/license-validation\";\nimport { licenseValidateRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"@/server/api/routers/download-ip\";\n\n/**\n * License Validation API\n *\n * Validates license keys against the database.\n * Supports both Polar-issued and Stripe-issued licenses.\n *\n * DEVICE TRACKING:\n * When device_id is provided, tracks the device validation in the\n * device_validations table for fair usage policy monitoring.\n *\n * BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:\n * - macOS app calls this endpoint with same request/response format\n * - device_id is optional - old apps without it still work\n *\n * EXTENDED RESPONSE (when include_credits=true):\n * - Returns credit balance for CF Workers to cache\n * - Used by HyperWhisper Cloud for usage-based billing\n *\n * CHECKS:\n * 1. License key exists in database\n * 2. Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n *\n * POLAR FALLBACK:\n * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n */\n\n/**\n * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n */\nasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n  if (!success) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 429 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Invalid request body\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name } =\n    (body ?? {}) as {\n      license_key?: string;\n      include_credits?: boolean;\n      device_id?: string;\n      device_name?: string;\n    };\n\n  if (!license_key) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"License key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Lookup in database, with Polar fallback + status check\n    const result = await checkLicenseKey(license_key);\n\n    if (!result.valid) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { valid: false, error: result.error },\n        { status: result.status }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const license = result.license;\n\n    // DEVICE TRACKING: Record device validation for fair usage monitoring\n    if (device_id) {\n      await trackDeviceValidation(license.id, device_id, device_name);\n    }\n\n    // License is valid - return extended info if requested\n    if (include_credits) {\n      const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        valid: true,\n        credits,\n        stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId || null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Basic response for macOS app compatibility\n    return NextResponse.json({ valid: true });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"License validation error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Failed to validate license. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"898c491c-6417-44ca-b8c4-74e3fe764e7a","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:53.164Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport {\n  upsertDeviceValidation,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { checkLicenseKey } from \"@/src/lib/license-validation\";\nimport { licenseValidateRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"@/server/api/routers/download-ip\";\n\n/**\n * License Validation API\n *\n * Validates license keys against the database.\n * Supports both Polar-issued and Stripe-issued licenses.\n *\n * DEVICE TRACKING:\n * When device_id is provided, tracks the device validation in the\n * device_validations table for fair usage policy monitoring.\n *\n * BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:\n * - macOS app calls this endpoint with same request/response format\n * - device_id is optional - old apps without it still work\n *\n * EXTENDED RESPONSE (when include_credits=true):\n * - Returns credit balance for CF Workers to cache\n * - Used by HyperWhisper Cloud for usage-based billing\n *\n * CHECKS:\n * 1. Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n *\n * POLAR FALLBACK:\n * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n */\n\n/**\n * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n */\nasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n  if (!success) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 429 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Invalid request body\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name } =\n    (body ?? {}) as {\n      license_key?: string;\n      include_credits?: boolean;\n      device_id?: string;\n      device_name?: string;\n    };\n\n  if (!license_key) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"License key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Lookup in database, with Polar fallback + status check\n    const result = await checkLicenseKey(license_key);\n\n    if (!result.valid) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { valid: false, error: result.error },\n        { status: result.status }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const license = result.license;\n\n    // DEVICE TRACKING: Record device validation for fair usage monitoring\n    if (device_id) {\n      await trackDeviceValidation(license.id, device_id, device_name);\n    }\n\n    // License is valid - return extended info if requested\n    if (include_credits) {\n      const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        valid: true,\n        credits,\n        stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId || null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Basic response for macOS app compatibility\n    return NextResponse.json({ valid: true });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"License validation error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Failed to validate license.    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, contentType, contentLength };\n}\n\nexport async function transcribeRoute(c: Context) {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const startTime = performance.now();\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  const flyRequestId = getFlyRequestId(c);\n\n  // IP block check\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'ip_blocked',\n      flyRequestId,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.ip_check_done', { flyRequestId });\n\n  const providerSelection = extractProvider(c);\n  if (!providerSelection.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_provider',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provided: providerSelection.provided,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT provider',\n      `Unknown X-STT-Provider \"${providerSelection.provided}\".`,\n      { requestId, provided: providerSelection.provided },\n    );\n  }\n  const provider = providerSelection.provider;\n\n  // Resolve + validate the requested model against the server-side registry.\n  // An unknown model for the provider is rejected (fail-closed) rather than\n    flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    flyMachineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID,\n    proxyOverheadMs,\n    provider,\n    model: model || 'default',\n    domain: domain || 'none',\n    contentType,\n    contentLength,\n    language: language || 'auto',\n    hasInitialPrompt: Boolean(initialPrompt),\n    mode: mode || 'default',\n  });\n\n  // Auth (query params only) — Cloud is licensed-only; a valid account key is required.\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param name; `license_key` is the legacy alias\n  // that installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({\n    licenseKey:\n      rawQuery(c.req.url, 'account_key') ?? rawQuery(c.req.url, 'license_key'),\n  });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'auth_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: authResult.response.status,\n    });\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.auth_done');\n\n  // Vocabulary surcharge: AssemblyAI charges a keyterms_prompt add-on (universal-3-pro)\n  // and ElevenLabs a +20% keyterm surcharge (scribe_v2) when an initial_prompt is supplied.\n  // We pass the raw hasInitialPrompt flag through to the reservation so it can reserve the\n  // surcharge for ANY eligible chain member — including ElevenLabs reached via a\n  // Deepgram/Groq/Grok fallback, which still forwards the prompt and bills the surcharge.\n  // estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on to universal-3-pro / scribe_v2, so passing it\n  // for every request is safe and never under-reserves.\n {"parentUuid":"a49c30fd-874d-4749-8a01-402c0e0cbd89","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a35f1a5a89ceb1af1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N78d31fnqTGTopbZTV4zy5","type":"tool_result","content":"import { afterEach, describe, expect, mock, test } from 'bun:test';\nimport { Hono } from 'hono';\n\nconst originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;\n\nconst cacheWrites: Array<{ licenseKey: string; license: { isValid: boolean; credits: number; cachedAt: string } }> = [];\n\nmock.module('../lib/redis', () => ({\n  getCachedLicense: async () => ({ isValid: true, credits: 12, cachedAt: 'cached' }),\n  cacheLicense: async (licenseKey: string, license: { isValid: boolean; credits: number; cachedAt: string }) => {\n    cacheWrites.push({ licenseKey, license });\n  },\n  isIPBlocked: async () => false,\n}));\n\nconst { readFiniteCredits, usageRoute } = await import('./usage');\n\nafterEach(() => {\n  cacheWrites.length = 0;\n  globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;\n});\n\ndescribe('readFiniteCredits', () => {\n  test('accepts only finite numeric credits', () => {\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({ credits: 12.5 })).toBe(12.5);\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({ credits: null })).toBeNull();\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({ credits: '12.5' })).toBeNull();\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({ credits: Number.NaN })).toBeNull();\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({ credits: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY })).toBeNull();\n    expect(readFiniteCredits({})).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('usageRoute force refresh', () => {\n  test('does not cache malformed credits balance responses and falls back to validation', async () => {\n    globalThis.fetch = mock(async (input: RequestInfo | URL) => {\n      const url = String(input);\n\n      if (url.includes('/api/license/credits?')) {\n        return Response.json({ credits: null });\n      }\n\n      if (url.includes('/api/license/validate')) {\n        return Response.json({ valid: true, credits: 42 });\n      }\n\n      throw new Error(`Unexpected fetch: ${url}`);\n    }) as unknown as typeof fetch;\n\n    const app = new Hono();\n    app.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n\n    const response = await app.request('/usage?license_key=test-license&force_refresh=true');\n    const body = await response.json() as { credits_remaining: number;
